```diff
+static void
+initconfig_free_wstr(wchar_t *member)
+{
+    if (member) {

+}
+
+
+static void
+initconfig_free_wstr_list(PyWideStringList *list)
+{
+    for (Py_ssize_t i = 0; i < list->length; i++) {
+        free(list->items[i]);
+    }
+    free(list->items);
+}
+
+
+static void
+initconfig_free_config(const PyConfig *config)
+{
+    const PyConfigSpec *spec = PYCONFIG_SPEC;
+    for (; spec->name != NULL; spec++) {
+        void *member = config_get_spec_member(config, spec);
+        if (spec->type == PyConfig_MEMBER_WSTR
+            || spec->type == PyConfig_MEMBER_WSTR_OPT)
+        {
+            wchar_t *wstr = *(wchar_t **)member;
+            initconfig_free_wstr(wstr);
+        }
+        else if (spec->type == PyConfig_MEMBER_WSTR_LIST) {
+            initconfig_free_wstr_list(member);
+        }
+    }
+}
```

```text
`PyConfig_SetString` uses `PyMem_Raw` rather than plain `malloc`. Can you mix those?
```

**vstinner**, 2025-09-11T10:08:16Z:

```diff

+{
+    if (member) {
+        free(member);
```

```text
PyConfig_SetString() function cannot be used with PEP 741 `PyInitConfig` API, you must use `PyInitConfig_SetStr()` which uses `malloc()`.
```

**encukou**, 2025-09-11T11:56:26Z:


### Original Issue

Source: [https://github.com/python/cpython/issues/121847](https://github.com/python/cpython/issues/121847)

````text
# Bug report

### Bug description:

When building Python with `--with-address-sanitizer` flag, the build fails. Leak sanitizer triggers when python is run.

Possible way to reproduce:
```bash
nix build --impure --expr \
  'with import <nixpkgs> {};
   python312.overrideAttrs (oldAttrs: {
     configureFlags = oldAttrs.configureFlags or [] ++ ["--with-address-sanitizer"];
   })'

```
````

```text
This particular leak looks cosmetic: `_bootstrap_python` is a build-only tool that doesn't run long enough for leaks to be a concern.

If send a fix for the `main` branch I can merge it, but we're unlikely to backport it to 3.12.
```

### Comment 9 — vstinner, 2025-09-10T15:17:56Z

Source: [https://github.com/python/cpython/issues/121847#issuecomment-3275435255](https://github.com/python/cpython/issues/121847#issuecomment-3275435255)

````text
Ah, without `--with-pydebug`, immortal interned strings are not released at Python exit. Extract of `_PyUnicode_ClearInterned()`:

```
        case SSTATE_INTERNED_IMMORTAL:
            /* Make immortal interned strings mortal again.
             *
             * Currently, the runtime is not able to guarantee that it can exit
             * without allocations that carry over to a future initialization
             * of Python within the same process. i.e:
             *   ./python -X showrefcount -c 'import itertools'
             *   [237 refs, 237 blocks]
             *
             * This should remain disabled (`Py_DEBUG` only) until there is a
             * strict guarantee that no memory will be left after
             * `Py_Finalize`.
             */
```

See issue gh-113993 and change GH-120520.
````
